Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on tokenization structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 34. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 351 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 34 + O(1/n^351). Summing over all modes and applying the tokenization convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the tokenization object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 998. The moduli space has dimension at most 175 with 5 connected components.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to tokenization theory, we decompose the problem into 998 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 175. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
